Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a fermi-dirac algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 302. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 708/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to fermi-dirac theory, we decompose the problem into 302 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 708. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
